Abstract: Every molecule ever synthesised can be drawn as a 2D skeletal diagram, yet in modern property prediction this universally available representation has received less focus in favour of molecular graphs, 3D conformers, or billion-parameter language models, each imposing its own computational and data-engineering overhead. We present $\textbf{MolSight}$, the first systematic large-scale study of vision-based Molecular Property Prediction (MPP). Using 10 vision architectures, 7 pre-training strategies, and $2\,M$ molecule images, we evaluate performance across 10 downstream tasks spanning physical-property regression, drug-discovery classification, and quantum-chemistry prediction. To account for the wide variation in structural complexity across pre-training molecules, we further propose a $\textbf{chemistry-informed curriculum}$: five structural complexity descriptors partition the corpus into five tiers of increasing chemical difficulty, consistently outperforming non-curriculum baselines. We show that a single rendered bond-line image, processed by a vision encoder, is sufficient for competitive molecular property prediction, i.e. $\textit{chemical insight from sight alone}$. The best curriculum-trained configuration achieves the top result on $\textbf{5 of 10}$ benchmarks and top two on $\textbf{all 10}$, at $\textbf{$\textit{80$\times$ lower}$}$ FLOPs than the nearest multi-modal competitor.

</details>


### [181] [Active-SAOOD: Active Sparsely Annotated Oriented Object Detection in Remote Sensing Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10162)
*Yu Lin,Jianghang Lin,Kai Ye,Shengchuan Zhang,Liujuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Active-SAOOD方法，通过主动学习在实例级别选择最有价值的稀疏标注样本，联合考虑朝向、分类、定位不确定性以及类内类间多样性，在仅1%标注比例下相比基线提升9%性能，有效降低了遥感场景中定向目标检测的标注成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少遥感领域中定向目标检测的标注成本仍是一大挑战。现有稀疏标注方法存在两个问题：(1)依赖类别相关采样；(2)缺乏对稀疏样本特性的深入研究，限制了其进一步发展。

Method: 提出Active-SAOOD方法，包含模型状态观测模块，通过联合考虑朝向不确定性、分类不确定性、定位不确定性以及类间和类内多样性，在实例级别主动选择最适合当前模型状态的最有价值稀疏样本。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，Active-SAOOD在各种随机稀疏标注下显著提升了现有SAOOD方法的性能和稳定性。仅用1%标注比例，即可获得比基线高9%的性能提升。

Conclusion: Active-SAOOD使稀疏标注定向目标检测能够在完全随机初始化的稀疏标注下稳定运行，扩展了其在更广泛实际场景中的适用性，进一步提升了SAOOD在遥感中的实用价值。

Abstract: Reducing the annotation cost of oriented object detection in remote sensing remains a major challenge. Recently, sparse annotation has gained attention for effectively reducing annotation redundancy in densely remote sensing scenes. However, (1) the sparse data reliance on class-dependent sampling, and (2) the lack of in-depth investigation into the characteristics of sparse samples hinders its further development. This paper proposes an active learning-based sparsely annotated oriented object detection (SAOOD) method, termed Active-SAOOD. Based on a model state observation module, Active-SAOOD actively selects the most valuable sparse samples at the instance level that are best suited to the current model state, by jointly considering orientation, classification, and localization uncertainty, as well as inter- and intra-class diversity. This design enables SAOOD to operate stably under completely randomly initialized sparse annotations and extends its applicability to broader real-world. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that Active-SAOOD significantly improves both performance and stability of existing SAOOD methods under various random sparse annotation. In particular, with only 1\% annotated ratios, it achieves a 9\% performance gain over the baseline, further enhancing the practical value of SAOOD in remote sensing. The code will be public.

</details>


### [182] [Task-Agnostic Noisy Label Detection via Standardized Loss Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10165)
*Inhyuk Park,Doohyun Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为标准化损失聚合（SLA）的统计驱动且任务无关的框架，用于在样本级别检测医学影像数据集中的噪声标签。SLA通过聚合多次交叉验证中标准化的折级验证损失来量化标签可靠性，将离散硬计数方案推广为连续估计器，在眼底数据集上优于硬计数基线，能有效识别模糊或错误标注的样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模医学影像数据集中普遍存在由于观察者间变异和模糊病例导致的噪声标签问题，需要一种能够有效检测并量化标签噪声的方法来提高数据集可靠性。

Method: SLA框架通过重复交叉验证运行，聚合标准化后的折级验证损失，将离散的硬计数方案推广为连续估计器，同时捕捉性能偏差的频率和幅度，生成可解释且统计稳定的噪声分数。

Result: 在公共眼底数据集上，SLA在所有噪声水平下均一致优于硬计数基线，且在低噪声比率下收敛速度显著更快，此时细微的损失变化具有信息量。

Conclusion: SLA能够有效检测潜在模糊或错误标注的样本，指导高效的重标注过程，提升任何分类任务的数据集可靠性。

Abstract: Noisy labels are common in large-scale medical imaging datasets due to inter-observer variability and ambiguous cases. We propose a statistically grounded and task-agnostic framework, Standardized Loss Aggregation (SLA), for detecting noisy labels at the sample level. SLA quantifies label reliability by aggregating standardized fold-level validation losses across repeated cross-validation runs. This formulation generalizes discrete hard-counting schemes into a continuous estimator that captures both the frequency and magnitude of performance deviations, yielding interpretable and statistically stable noisiness scores. Experiments on a public fundus dataset demonstrate that SLA consistently outperforms the hard-counting baseline across all noise levels and converges substantially faster, especially under low noise ratios where subtle loss variations are informative. Samples with high SLA scores indicate potentially ambiguous or mislabeled cases, guiding efficient re-annotation and improving dataset reliability for any classification task.

</details>


### [183] [V-ABS: Action-Observer Driven Beam Search for Dynamic Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10172)
*Zhiwei Ning,Xuanang Gao,Jiaxi Cao,Gengming Zhang,Shengnan Ma,Wenwen Tong,Hanming Deng,Jie Yang,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出V-ABS，一种基于行动-观察者驱动的波束搜索框架，通过思考者-行动者-观察者迭代和熵权自适应加权算法，有效缓解多模态大语言模型在多步视觉推理中的想象-行动-观察者偏差，在8个基准上平均提升19.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在多步视觉推理中存在想象-行动-观察者（IAO）偏差，即先验想象与执行反馈之间的错位，导致推理不稳定且非最优，亟需解决这一偏差问题。

Method: 提出V-ABS框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）思考者-行动者-观察者迭代的波束搜索机制；（2）基于熵的适应性加权算法，动态平衡策略先验与观察反馈的置信度；（3）构建包含8万+样本的大规模SFT数据集，引导模型为正确行动路径赋予更高先验置信度。

Result: 在8个多样化基准上取得SOTA性能，在Qwen3-VL-8B基线上平均提升19.7%，且对开源和闭源模型均有稳定提升。

Conclusion: V-ABS通过显式建模行动与观察反馈的交互，有效缓解了IAO偏差，提升了多步视觉推理的稳定性和最优性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable success in general perception, yet complex multi-step visual reasoning remains a persistent challenge. Although recent agentic approaches incorporate tool use, they often neglect critical execution feedback. Consequently, they suffer from the imagination-action-observer (IAO) bias, a misalignment between prior imagination and observer feedback that undermines reasoning stability and optimality. To bridge this gap, we introduce V-ABS, an action-observer driven beam search framework that enables deliberate reasoning through thinker-actor-observer iterations. We also propose an entropy-based adaptive weighting algorithm to mitigate the IAO bias by dynamically balancing the confidence scores between the policy priors and the observational feedback. Moreover, we construct a large-scale supervised fine-tuning (SFT) dataset comprising over 80k samples to guide the model to assign higher prior confidence to correct action paths. Extensive experiments across eight diverse benchmarks show that V-ABS achieves state-of-the-art performance, delivering an average improvement of 19.7% on the Qwen3-VL-8B baseline and consistent gains across both open-source and proprietary models.

</details>


### [184] [MTA-RL: Robust Urban Driving via Multi-modal Transformer-based 3D Affordances and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10177)
*Guangli Chen,Dianzhao Li,Wenjian Zhong,Bangquan Xie,Ostap Okhrin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MTA-RL 提出首个通过多模态Transformer预测3D可驾驶性表征、再结合强化学习进行决策的端到端框架，在CARLA仿真中实现SOTA性能与零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端模型缺乏可解释性，模块化管线又存在错误传播问题，亟需一种兼具可解释性与鲁棒性的感知-决策桥接方案。

Method: 使用Transformer架构融合RGB图像与LiDAR点云，预测显式的几何感知可驾驶性表征（affordances）；将结构化表征作为紧凑观测空间，输入RL策略进行决策。

Result: 在CARLA Town01-03、20-60辆背景车密度下全面超越SOTA；Town03训练后零样本迁移，Route Completion提升9.0%，Total Distance提升11.0%，Distance Per Violation提升83.7%。

Conclusion: 多模态融合与奖励塑造至关重要；MTA-RL通过结构化可驾驶性表征有效提升了样本效率、稳定性和泛化能力，适用于鲁棒的城市自动驾驶。

Abstract: Robust urban autonomous driving requires reliable 3D scene understanding and stable decision-making under dense interactions. However, existing end-to-end models lack interpretability, while modular pipelines suffer from error propagation across brittle interfaces. This paper proposes MTA-RL, the first framework that bridges perception and control through Multi-modal Transformer-based 3D Affordances and Reinforcement Learning (RL). Unlike previous fusion models that directly regress actions, RGB images and LiDAR point clouds are fused using a transformer architecture to predict explicit, geometry-aware affordance representations. These structured representations serve as a compact observation space, enabling the RL policy to operate purely on predicted driving semantics, which significantly improves sample efficiency and stability. Extensive evaluations in CARLA Town01-03 across varying densities (20-60 background vehicles) show that MTA-RL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Trained solely on Town03, our method demonstrates superior zero-shot generalization in unseen towns, achieving up to a 9.0% increase in Route Completion, an 11.0% increase in Total Distance, and an 83.7% improvement in Distance Per Violation. Furthermore, ablation studies confirm that our multi-modal fusion and reward shaping are critical, significantly outperforming image-only and unshaped variants, demonstrating the effectiveness of MTA-RL for robust urban autonomous driving.

</details>


### [185] [What Concepts Lie Within? Detecting and Suppressing Risky Content in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10180)
*Chenyu Zhang,Lanjun Wang,Yueyang Cheng,Ruidong Chen,Wenhui Li,An-an Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AHV-D&S，一种无需训练、推理时动态检测并抑制DiT文本到图像模型中注意力头敏感内容的方法，通过注意力头向量（AHV）量化文本token敏感性，并采用动量跟踪与自适应抑制策略，有效去除性、暴力、版权等风险内容，同时保持生成质量与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险概念消除方法主要针对早期U-Net架构，无法有效保护基于Diffusion Transformer（DiT）的最新T2I模型，因为DiT通过联合注意力机制将语义注入与视觉合成纠缠在一起，难以隔离和擦除风险内容。

Method: 1. 发现DiT中注意力头具有概念特异性敏感性；2. 提出注意力头向量（AHV）作为检测风险生成倾向的判别性签名；3. 在推理阶段采用动量策略动态跟踪token级AHV；4. 基于头特异性风险分数进行敏感性引导的自适应注意力权重抑制。

Result: AHV-D&S有效抑制色情、版权风格及各种有害内容，同时保持视觉质量，并对对抗性提示具有强鲁棒性，在不同DiT-based T2I模型间表现出良好的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 基于DiT注意力头概念特异性敏感性的发现，AHV-D&S作为训练无关的推理时保护机制，填补了现有方法在Diffusion Transformer架构下的安全防护空白。

Abstract: The rise of text-to-image (T2I) models has increasingly raised concerns regarding the generation of risky content, such as sexual, violent, and copyright-protected images, highlighting the need for effective safeguards within the models themselves. Although existing methods have been proposed to eliminate risky concepts from T2I models, they are primarily developed for earlier U-Net architectures, leaving the state-of-the-art Diffusion-Transformer-based T2I models inadequately protected. This gap stems from a fundamental architectural shift: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) entangle semantic injection and visual synthesis via joint attention, which makes it difficult to isolate and erase risky content within the generation. To bridge this gap, we investigate how semantic concepts are represented in DiTs and discover that attention heads exhibit concept-specific sensitivity. This property enables both the detection and suppression of risky content. Building on this discovery, we propose AHV-D\&S, a training-free inference-time safeguard for image generation in DiTs. Specifically, AHV-D\&S quantifies each textual token's sensitivity across all attention heads as an Attention Head Vector (AHV), which serves as a discriminative signature for detecting risky generation tendencies. In the inference stage, we propose a momentum-based strategy to dynamically track token-wise AHVs across denoising steps, and a sensitivity-guided adaptive suppression strategy that suppresses the attention weights of identified risky tokens based on head-specific risk scores. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AHV-D\&S effectively suppresses sexual, copyrighted-style, and various harmful content while preserving visual quality, and further exhibits strong robustness against adversarial prompts and transferability across different DiT-based T2I models.

</details>


### [186] [A Comparative Study of Machine Learning and Deep Learning for Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10181)
*Jihyeon Baek,Seunghoon Lee,Gitaek Kwon,Doohyun Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在眼底图像OOD检测任务中，轻量级机器学习方法与深度学习方法达到同等性能（AUROC=1.000，准确率0.999-1.000），但ML方法具有显著更低的端到端延迟和更高的计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习常被认为优于传统机器学习，但医学影像在标准化采集协议下视觉变异性有限，因此有必要直接比较两种方法在OOD检测任务上的表现。

Method: 在超过60,000张眼底和非眼底图像（多分辨率）的开放数据集上，分别使用ML和DL方法进行OOD检测，并在内部和外部验证集上评估性能。

Result: 两种方法均达到AUROC 1.000和准确率0.999-1.000；ML方法的端到端延迟显著低于DL方法。

Conclusion: 对于视觉复杂度有限的OOD检测任务，轻量级ML方法可在达到DL水平性能的同时显著降低计算成本，更利于实际部署。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is essential for building reliable AI systems, as models that produce outputs for invalid inputs cannot be trusted. Although deep learning (DL) is often assumed to outperform traditional machine learning (ML), medical imaging data are typically acquired under standardized protocols, leading to relatively constrained image variability in OOD detection tasks. This motivates a direct comparison between ML and DL approaches in this setting. The two approaches are evaluated on open datasets comprising over 60,000 fundus and non-fundus images across multiple resolutions. Both approaches achieved an AUROC of 1.000 and accuracies between 0.999 and 1.000 on internal and external validation sets, showing comparable detection performance. The ML approach, however, exhibited substantially lower end-to-end latency while maintaining equivalent accuracy, indicating greater computational efficiency. These results suggest that for OOD detection tasks of limited visual complexity, lightweight ML approaches can achieve DL-level performance with significantly reduced computational cost, supporting practical real-world deployment.

</details>


### [187] [Developing a foundation model for high-resolution remote sensing data of the Netherlands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10184)
*Paul Vermeeren,Heysem Kaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文基于荷兰120万张高分辨率卫星图像，通过结合CNN和Vision Transformer构建基础模型，利用时序数据学习时空依赖特征，在植被监测等下游任务中表现优异，且以更少参数在全球基准上达到竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感基础模型通常依赖大规模全球数据，计算成本高；同时单时相数据存在特征模糊性，难以区分相似地物。作者旨在探索是否可利用有限区域数据（仅荷兰）结合时序信息，学习通用且可迁移的遥感表征。

Method: 采用CNN与Vision Transformer混合架构以同时捕获低频和高频景观特征；利用时序卫星影像作为输入，通过时间依赖关系（地形特征、土地覆盖变化、季节动态）增强表征学习；在仅荷兰区域数据上预训练，随后迁移至全球基准测试。

Result: 在荷兰植被监测数据集上，引入时序信息相比单时相方法性能显著提升；尽管模型更小、预训练数据仅限荷兰，但在全球基准上达到了与更大规模SOTA遥感模型竞争的性能。

Conclusion: 有限区域数据结合时序信息可以学习丰富且可泛化的遥感表征，以更少参数实现全局竞争力，证明了数据效率与模型小型化的可行性。

Abstract: We develop a foundation model using 1.2m high resolution satellite images of the Netherlands. By combining a Convolutional Neural Network and a Vision Transformer, the model captures both low- and high-frequency landscape features, such as fine textures, edges, and small objects as well as large terrain structures, elevation patterns, and land-cover distributions. Leveraging temporal data as input, the model learns from broader contextual information across time, allowing the model to exploit the temporal dependencies, such as topographic features, land-cover changes, and seasonal dynamics. These additional constraints reduce feature ambiguity, improve representation learning, and enable better generalization with fewer labeled samples. The foundation model is evaluated on multiple downstream tasks, ranging from use cases within the Netherlands to global benchmarking datasets. On the vegetation monitoring dataset of the Netherlands, the model shows clear performance improvements by incorporating temporal information instead of relying on a single time point. Despite using a smaller model and less pretraining data limited to the Netherlands, it achieves competitive results on global benchmarks when compared to state-of-the-art models. These results demonstrate that the model can learn rich, generalizable representations from limited data, achieving competitive performance on global benchmarks while using a fraction of the parameters of larger state-of-the-art remote sensing models. To maximize reproducibility and reuse, we made the scripts and the model accessible on GitHub.

</details>


### [188] [DynGhost: Temporally-Modelled Transformer for Dynamic Ghost Imaging with Quantum Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10185)
*Vittorio Palladino,Ahmet Enis Cetin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DynGhost，一种基于Transformer架构的动态鬼成像方法，通过交替时空注意力块和量子感知训练框架（含Anscombe方差稳定归一化），同时解决动态场景时间相干性利用不足和真实单光子硬件泊松统计特性模拟不准两个关键问题，在动态和光子匮乏场景中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习鬼成像方法存在两个未解决的关键局限：一是无法利用帧间时间相干性，动态鬼成像问题基本未解决；二是假设加性高斯噪声模型，不符合真实单光子硬件（SNSPDs、SPADs、SiPMs）的泊松统计特性。

Method: 提出DynGhost（动态鬼成像Transformer），架构上采用交替的空间注意力块和时间注意力块；训练框架为量子感知训练，基于物理精确的探测器模拟和Anscombe方差稳定归一化变换，解决分布偏移问题。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，DynGhost在动态场景和光子匮乏条件下，性能优于传统重建方法和现有深度学习架构。

Conclusion: 通过Transformer架构同时解决动态鬼成像的时间相干性利用问题和真实硬件噪声建模问题，为动态和光子匮乏场景提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Ghost imaging reconstructs spatial information from a single-pixel bucket detector by correlating structured illumination patterns with scalar intensity measurements. While deep learning approaches have achieved promising results on static scenes, two critical limitations remain unaddressed: existing architectures fail to exploit temporal coherence across frames, leaving dynamic ghost imaging largely unsolved, and they assume additive Gaussian noise models that do not reflect the true Poissonian statistics of real single-photon hardware. We present DynGhost (Dynamic Ghost Imaging Transformer), a transformer architecture that addresses both limitations through alternating spatial and temporal attention blocks. Our quantum-aware training framework, based on physically accurate detector simulations (SNSPDs, SPADs, SiPMs) and Anscombe variance-stabilizing normalization, resolves the distribution shift that causes classical models to fail under realistic hardware constraints. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that DynGhost outperforms both traditional reconstruction methods and existing deep learning architectures, with particular gains in dynamic and photon-starved settings.

</details>


### [189] [SciVQR: A Multidisciplinary Multimodal Benchmark for Advanced Scientific Reasoning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10187)
*Longteng Guo,Xuanxu Lin,Dongze Hao,Tongtian Yue,Pengkang Huo,Jiatong Ma,Yuchen Liu,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SciVQR是一个覆盖数学、物理、化学、地理、天文学和生物学54个细分领域的多模态科学推理基准，包含领域特定的视觉元素（公式、图表、图示），任务涵盖从事实回忆到多步推理，46%的题目附有专家撰写的解答过程，旨在评估MLLMs的推理过程而不仅仅是最终答案。实验表明现有MLLMs（包括闭源和开源）在复杂多模态推理任务中存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）基准测试未能充分捕捉科学推理所需的多模态输入整合、领域专业知识以及跨学科多步推理过程的复杂性和可追溯性，因此需要一个新的基准来严格评估模型的推理能力。

Method: 构建了覆盖6大学科54个细分领域的SciVQR多模态基准，包含领域特定视觉元素（公式、图表、图示），46%的题目提供专家撰写的解答过程，不仅评估最终答案还分析推理过程。

Result: 对主流MLLMs（包括闭源和开源模型）的评估显示，这些模型在处理复杂多模态推理任务时存在显著局限性。

Conclusion: 现有MLLMs在多步推理和跨学科知识整合方面仍需改进，以向真正的科学智能迈进。

Abstract: Scientific reasoning is a key aspect of human intelligence, requiring the integration of multimodal inputs, domain expertise, and multi-step inference across various subjects. Existing benchmarks for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often fail to capture the complexity and traceability of reasoning processes necessary for rigorous evaluation. To fill this gap, we introduce SciVQR, a multimodal benchmark covering 54 subfields in mathematics, physics, chemistry, geography, astronomy, and biology. SciVQR includes domain-specific visuals, such as equations, charts, and diagrams, and challenges models to combine visual comprehension with reasoning. The tasks range from basic factual recall to complex, multi-step inferences, with 46% including expert-authored solutions. SciVQR not only evaluates final answers but also examines the reasoning process, providing insights into how models reach their conclusions. Our evaluation of leading MLLMs, including both proprietary and open-source models, reveals significant limitations in handling complex multimodal reasoning tasks, underscoring the need for improved multi-step reasoning and better integration of interdisciplinary knowledge in advancing MLLMs toward true scientific intelligence. The dataset and evaluation code are publicly available at https://github.com/CASIA-IVA-Lab/SciVQR.

</details>


### [190] [DetRefiner: Model-Agnostic Detection Refinement with Feature Fusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10190)
*Soichiro Okazaki,Tatsuya Sasaki,Hiroki Ohashi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DetRefiner 是一种即插即用的轻量级框架，通过融合全局与局部特征（来自 DINOv3 等基础模型）来校准开放词汇目标检测器的置信度，无需访问检测器内部特征或重新训练，在多个数据集上显著提升了新类别的检测性能（最高 +10.1 AP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇目标检测方法因对全局与局部上下文线索的整合有限，难以泛化到未见类别。

Method: 利用轻量级 Transformer 编码器融合来自基础模型（如 DINOv3）的全局图像特征和局部 patch 特征，生成类向量和 patch 向量，从中推断属性可靠性并重新校准基检测器的置信度。DetRefiner 独立于基检测器训练，推理时仅依赖基检测器的预测结果，通过合并校准分数得到最终置信度。

Result: 在 COCO、LVIS、ODinW13 和 Pascal VOC 数据集上，DetRefiner 一致提升了多种 OVOD 模型，在新类别上最高提升 +10.1 AP。

Conclusion: 学习融合全局与局部表示为推进开放世界目标检测提供了一种强大且通用的机制。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection (OVOD) aims to detect both seen and unseen categories, yet existing methods often struggle to generalize to novel objects due to limited integration of global and local contextual cues. We propose DetRefiner, a simple yet effective plug-and-play framework that learns to fuse global and local features to refine open-vocabulary detection. DetRefiner processes global image features and patch-level image features from foundational models (e.g., DINOv3) through a lightweight Transformer encoder. The encoder produces a class vector capturing image-level attributes and patch vectors representing local region attributes, from which attribute reliability is inferred to recalibrate the base model's confidence. Notably, DetRefiner is trained independently of the base OVOD model, requiring neither access to its internal features nor retraining. At inference, it operates solely on the base detector's predictions, producing auxiliary calibration scores that are merged with the base detector's scores to yield the final refined confidence. Despite this simplicity, DetRefiner consistently enhances multiple OVOD models across COCO, LVIS, ODinW13, and Pascal VOC, achieving gains of up to +10.1 AP on novel categories. These results highlight that learning to fuse global and local representations offers a powerful and general mechanism for advancing open-world object detection. Our codes and models are available at https://github.com/hitachi-rd-cv/detrefiner.

</details>


### [191] [3DReflecNet: A Large-Scale Dataset for 3D Reconstruction of Reflective, Transparent, and Low-Texture Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10204)
*Zhicheng Liang,Haoyi Yu,Boyan Li,Dayou Zhang,Zijian Cao,Tianyi Gong,Junhua Liu,Shuguang Cui,Fangxin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了3DReflecNet，一个超过22TB的大型混合数据集，包含12万+合成实例和1000+真实物体，专门用于评估和推动反射、透明、低纹理等挑战性材质的3D重建方法。实验表明现有方法在这些场景下性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D重建数据集主要聚焦于漫反射、有纹理的物体，对于反射、透明、低纹理等真实世界中常见的复杂材质缺乏覆盖，导致现有方法在这些场景下的性能评估不足。

Method: 构建了包含超过22TB数据的大型混合数据集3DReflecNet，其中包含超过12万合成实例（基于12,000+形状的物理渲染）和超过1,000个真实物体（使用消费级设备采集），总计超过700万张多视角图像。数据集覆盖多样材质、复杂光照条件和各种几何形态，包括从真实图像和LLM合成图像通过扩散管线生成的形状。设计了五个核心任务的基准测试：图像匹配、运动恢复结构、新视角合成、反射去除和重光照。

Result: 实验表明，现有最先进的3D重建方法在这些挑战性材质场景下难以保持准确性，性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 需要开发更具鲁棒性的3D视觉模型来应对真实世界中复杂材质的3D重建挑战。

Abstract: Accurate 3D reconstruction of objects with reflective, transparent, or low-texture surfaces still remains notoriously challenging. Such materials often violate key assumptions in multi-view reconstruction pipelines, such as photometric consistency and the availability on distinct geometric texture cues. Existing datasets primarily focus on diffuse, textured objects, and therefore provide limited insight into performance under real-world material complexities. We introduce 3DReflecNet, a large-scale hybrid dataset exceeding 22 TB that is specifically designed to benchmark and advance 3D vision methods for these challenging materials. 3DReflecNet combines two types of data: over 120,000 synthetic instances generated via physically-based rendering of more than 12,000 shapes, and over 1,000 real-world objects captured using consumer devices. Together, these data consist of more than 7 million multi-view frames. The dataset spans diverse materials, complex lighting conditions, and a wide range of geometric forms, including shapes generated from both real and LLM-synthesized 2D images using diffusion-based pipelines. To support robust evaluation, we design benchmarks for five core tasks: image matching, structure-from-motion, novel view synthesis, reflection removal, and relighting. Extensive experiments demonstrate that state-of-the-art methods struggle to maintain accuracy across these settings, highlighting the need for more resilient 3D vision models.

</details>


### [192] [VPD-100K: Towards Generalizable and Fine-grained Visual Privacy Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10229)
*Xiaobin Hu,Enpu Zuo,Lanping Hu,Kaiwen Yang,Dianshu Liao,Tianyi Zhang,Bo Yin,Yinsi Zhou,Shidong Pan,Xiaoyu Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个大规模、细粒度的视觉隐私数据集VPD-100K，包含10万张图像、33个细粒度类别和超过19万个对象实例，覆盖人类存在、屏幕PII、物理标识符和位置指示器四大隐私领域。同时设计了一个频率增强的轻量级模块，通过频域注意力融合和自适应谱门控机制捕捉敏感信息的细微细节，在图像和流媒体视频基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私检测模型受限于缺乏全面数据集：现有隐私数据集规模小、注释粗糙、领域覆盖窄，无法捕捉真实环境中敏感信息的复杂细节，严重阻碍了鲁棒检测模型的发展。

Method: 1) 构建VPD-100K数据集：包含100,000张图像，33个细粒度类别，超过190,000个对象实例，覆盖四大隐私领域（人类存在、屏幕PII、物理标识符、位置指示器）；2) 设计频率增强轻量级模块：包含频域注意力融合和自适应谱门控机制，突破空间像素强度限制以捕捉敏感信息细微细节。

Result: 统计显示数据集具有长尾分布、小目标尺度、高视觉复杂性的特性。在多种图像和流媒体视频基准上的实验一致证明了VPD-100K数据集及频率机制的有效性。

Conclusion: VPD-100K数据集因其大规模、细粒度注释和高视觉复杂性，对直播等无约束应用中频繁发生的无意识实时信息泄露场景具有重要价值；所提出的频率增强模块有效提升了隐私检测性能。

Abstract: Privacy protection has become a critical requirement in the era of ubiquitous visual data sharing, imposing higher demands on efficient and robust privacy detection algorithms. However, current robust detection models are severely hindered by the lack of comprehensive datasets. Existing privacy-oriented datasets often suffer from limited scale, coarse-grained annotations, and narrow domain coverage, failing to capture the intricate details of sensitive information in realworld environments. To bridge this gap, we present a large-scale, fine-grained Visual Privacy Dataset (VPD-100K), designed to facilitate generalized privacy detection. We establish a holistic taxonomy comprising four primary domains: Human Presence, On-Screen Personally Identifiable Information (PII), Physical Identifiers, and Location Indicators, containing 100,000 images annotated with 33 fine-grained classes and over 190,000 object instances. Statistical analysis reveals that our dataset features long-tailed distributions, small object scales, and high visual complexity. These characteristics make the dataset particularly valuable for demanding, unconstrained applications such as live streaming, where actors frequently face unintentional, realtime information leakage. Furthermore, we design an effective frequency-enhanced lightweight module consisting of frequency-domain attention fusion and adaptive spectral gating mechanism that breaks the limitations of spatial pixel intensity to better capture the subtle details of sensitive information. Extensive experiments conducted on both diverse image and streaming videos benchmarks consistently demonstrate the effectiveness of our VPD-100K dataset and the wellcurated frequency mechanism. The code and dataset are available at https://vpd-100k.github.io/.

</details>


### [193] [PolarVSR: A Unified Framework and Benchmark for Continuous Space-Time Polarization Video Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10275)
*Chenggong Li,Yidong Luo,Junchao Zhang,Boxin Shi,Degui Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出时空偏振视频重建架构，利用偏振感知隐式神经表示实现连续高保真上采样，并通过流引导偏振变化损失监督动态过程，同时建立了首个大规模彩色DoFP偏振视频基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Division-of-Focal-Plane (DoFP)彩色偏振成像在从马赛克阵列中恢复偏振参数时面临逆问题挑战，且硬件瓶颈导致无法支持高帧率采集，限制了动态视频任务中的偏振成像应用，因此需要联合时空增强。

Method: 首次提出时空偏振视频重建架构，联合建模偏振方向的空间和时间维度，使用偏振感知隐式神经表示实现连续高保真上采样，并通过分析偏振参数的时间变化引入流引导偏振变化损失来监督偏振动态。

Result: 建立了首个大规模彩色DoFP偏振视频基准，在该基准上的大量实验证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的时空偏振视频重建架构有效解决了DoFP彩色偏振成像中的逆问题及硬件帧率瓶颈，实现了动态偏振视频的高质量重建。

Abstract: Polarimetric imaging captures surface polarization characteristics, such as the Degree of Linear Polarization (DoLP) and the Angle of Polarization (AoP). In mainstream Division of-Focal-Plane (DoFP) color polarization imaging, recovering polarization parameters from captured mosaic arrays remains a challenging inverse problem. Existing DoFP cameras also face hardware bottlenecks and often cannot support high-frame-rate acquisition, limiting polarimetric imaging in dynamic video tasks. These limitations motivate joint spatial and temporal enhancement. To this end, we propose the first space-time polarization video reconstruction architecture. The method jointly models polarization directions in space and time and uses a polarization-aware implicit neural representation for continuous, high-fidelity upsampling. By analyzing temporal variations in polarization parameters, we further introduce a flow-guided polarization variation loss to supervise polarization dynamics. We also establish the first large-scale color DoFP polarization video benchmark to support this research direction. Extensive experiments on this benchmark demonstrate the effectiveness of the method.

</details>


### [194] [PaMoSplat: Part-Aware Motion-Guided Gaussian Splatting for Dynamic Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10307)
*Yinan Deng,Jianyu Dou,Jiahui Wang,Jingyu Zhao,Yi Yang,Yufeng Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PaMoSplat提出了一种结合部件感知与运动先验的动态高斯溅射框架，通过图聚类将多视图分割掩码提升至3D空间构建高斯部件，并利用差分进化算法与光流估计部件刚体运动，实现高质量动态场景重建与跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于3DGS的动态场景方法在处理大幅、复杂运动时难以同时保证高保真渲染与精确跟踪，亟需一种能够利用部件级先验与运动线索来提升动态场景建模质量的方法。

Method: 1) 通过图聚类将多视图分割掩码提升至3D空间，构建一致的刚体高斯部件；2) 利用差分进化算法结合多视图光流线索估计部件刚体运动，提供鲁棒优化初始值；3) 引入自适应迭代计数机制、内部可学习刚性与流监督渲染损失以加速训练。

Result: 在多种场景（包括真实环境）上的综合评估表明，PaMoSplat在渲染质量、跟踪精度和收敛速度上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: PaMoSplat有效解决了大幅复杂运动下的动态场景重建难题，并支持4D场景编辑等部件级下游应用。

Abstract: Dynamic scene reconstruction represents a fundamental yet demanding challenge in computer vision and robotics. While recent progress in 3DGS-based methods has advanced dynamic scene modeling, obtaining high-fidelity rendering and accurate tracking in scenarios with substantial, intricate motions remains significantly challenging. To address these challenges, we propose PaMoSplat, a novel dynamic Gaussian splatting framework incorporating part awareness and motion priors. Our approach is grounded in two key observations: 1) Parts serve as primitives for scene deformation, and 2) Motion cues from optical flow can effectively guide part motion. Specifically, PaMoSplat initializes by lifting multi-view segmentation masks into 3D space via graph clustering, establishing coherent Gaussian parts. For subsequent timestamps, we leverage a differential evolutionary algorithm to estimate the rigid motion of these parts using multi-view optical flow cues, providing a robust warm-start for further optimization. Additionally, PaMoSplat introduces an adaptive iteration count mechanism, internal learnable rigidity, and flow-supervised rendering loss to accelerate and optimize the training process. Comprehensive evaluations across diverse scenes, including real-world environments, demonstrate that PaMoSplat delivers superior rendering quality, improved tracking precision, and faster convergence compared to existing methods. Furthermore, it enables multiple part-level downstream applications, such as 4D scene editing.

</details>


### [195] [LimeCross: Context-Conditioned Layered Image Editing with Structural Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10319)
*Ryugo Morita,Stanislav Frolov,Brian Bernhard Moser,Ko Watanabe,Riku Takahashi,Issey Sukeda,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LimeCross是一种无需训练的上下文条件化分层图像编辑框架，通过双流注意力机制保持跨层一致性，并引入LayerEditBench基准（1500个分层场景），在层纯度和合成真实性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层图像编辑面临两大挑战：手动编辑需跨层协调光照和接触一致性；AI方法将分层图展平编辑后再分解，导致前后景泄漏和不稳定的透明度。需要一种能直接编辑RGBA层同时保持跨层一致性的方法。

Method: 提出LimeCross框架，采用双流注意力机制从其他层获取上下文线索以保持跨层一致性，并显式维护层完整性防止编辑层污染。该方法无需训练，直接根据文本编辑用户选中的RGBA层，其余层保持不变。

Result: 构建LayerEditBench基准（1500个分层场景及成对源/目标提示）和评估协议（编辑保真度+Alpha通道稳定性）。实验表明LimeCross在层纯度和合成真实性上显著优于强编辑基线方法。

Conclusion: LimeCross确立了上下文条件化分层编辑作为可控生成式创作的原则性框架，解决了交叉泄漏和不稳定透明度等关键问题。

Abstract: Layered image assets are widely used in real-world creative workflows, enabling non-destructive iteration and flexible re-composition. Recent advances in layered image generation and decomposition synthesize or recover layered representations, yet controllable editing of layered images remains challenging. Manual editing requires careful coordination across layers to maintain consistent illumination and contact, while AI-based pipelines collapse layers into a flattened image for editing, then decompose them again, introducing background-to-foreground leakage and unstable transparency. To address these limitations, we propose LimeCross, a training-free context-conditioned layered image editing framework that edits user-selected RGBA layers according to text while keeping the remaining layers unchanged. It leverages contextual cues from other layers using a bi-stream attention mechanism to preserve cross-layer consistency, while explicitly maintaining layer integrity to prevent the contamination of edited layers. To evaluate our approach, we introduce LayerEditBench, a benchmark of 1500 layered scenes with paired source/target prompts, along with evaluation protocols that assess both edit fidelity and alpha channel stability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LimeCross improves layer purity and composite realism over strong editing baselines, establishing context-conditioned layered editing as a principled framework for controllable generative creation.

</details>


### [196] [The Alpha Blending Hypothesis: Compositing Shortcut in Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10334)
*Andrii Yermakov,Jan Cech,Mario Fritz,Jiri Matas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Alpha Blending假说，认为现有深度伪造检测器本质上是alpha混合搜索器，通过定位低层次混合伪影而非语义异常或生成指纹来检测伪造。基于此提出BlenD方法，仅使用真实人脸图像经自混合增强训练，在15个数据集上取得最佳跨数据集泛化，集成模型AUROC达94.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测方法在跨数据集泛化上取得进展，但其底层机制尚不明确，缺乏对检测器究竟学习到何种特征的深入理解。

Method: 提出Alpha Blending Hypothesis假说，认为检测器主要学习定位面部融合时引入的低水平合成伪影；构建BlenD方法，仅用真实人脸图像通过自混合图像(SBI)增强进行训练，无需显式生成深度伪造样本。

Result: BlenD在15个深度伪造数据集（2019-2025）上取得最佳平均跨数据集泛化性能；显式混合搜索器与对混合捷径鲁棒的模型互补，集成后AUROC达94.0%。

Conclusion: 深度伪造检测器的工作机制可归结为对alpha混合伪影的搜索，利用这一发现可设计更高效、泛化性更强的检测方法，同时混合搜索器与鲁棒模型的互补性为提升检测性能提供了新方向。

Abstract: Recent deepfake detection methods demonstrate improved cross-dataset generalization, yet the underlying mechanisms remain underexplored. We introduce the Alpha Blending Hypothesis, positing that state-of-the-art frame-based detectors primarily function as alpha blending searchers; rather than learning semantic anomalies or specific generative neural fingerprints, they localize low-level compositing artifacts introduced during the integration of manipulated faces into target frames. We experimentally validate the hypothesis, demonstrating that deepfake detectors exhibit high sensitivity to the so-called self-blended images (SBI) and non-generative manipulations. We propose the method BlenD that leverages a large-scale, diverse dataset of real-only facial images augmented with SBI. This approach achieves the best average cross-dataset generalization on 15 compositional deepfake datasets released between 2019 and 2025 without utilizing explicitly generated deepfakes during training. Furthermore, we show that predictions from explicit blending searchers and models resilient to blending shortcuts are highly complementary, yielding a state-of-the-art AUROC of 94.0% in an ensemble configuration. The code with experiments and the trained model will be publicly released.

</details>


### [197] [EvoStreaming: Your Offline Video Model Is a Natively Streaming Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10343)
*Zichen Wen,Boxue Yang,Junlong Ke,Jiajie Huang,Chenfei Liao,Junxi Wang,Xuyang Liu,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了RealStreamEval评估协议和EvoStreaming自适应框架，通过仅1000个自生成样本（比主流方法少139倍）使VideoLLM学会流式交互策略，在五个开源模型上提升最高10.8分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VideoLLM主要面向离线推理，而流式视频理解需要模型在实时场景中自主决定何时响应，但现有流式基准将时序决策外化给评估者，缺乏对模型交互策略的评估。

Method: 1) 提出RealStreamEval帧级多轮评估协议，暴露序列观测并惩罚非必要响应；2) 提出EvoStreaming框架，让基础模型自身作为数据生成器、相关性标注器和roll-out策略，自合成流式轨迹，无需外部监督。

Result: 仅用1000个自生成样本（比主流流式指令微调方法少139倍），无需架构改动，在Qwen2/2.5/3-VL、InternVL-3.5、MiniCPM-V4.5五个开源VideoLLM上，RealStreamEval总分最高提升10.8分，同时基本保持离线视频性能。

Conclusion: 数据高效的交互策略微调是将现有VideoLLM适配为流式助手的一条实用路径。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding demands more than watching longer videos: assistants must decide when to speak in real time, balancing responsiveness against verbosity. Yet most video-language models (VideoLLMs) are trained for offline inference, and existing streaming benchmarks externalize this timing decision to the evaluator. We address this gap with RealStreamEval, a frame-level multi-turn evaluation protocol that exposes models to sequential observations and penalizes unnecessary responses. Under this protocol, we observed that strong offline VideoLLMs retain useful visual understanding but lack an interaction policy for deciding when to respond. Motivated by this observation, we propose EvoStreaming, a self-evolved streaming adaptation framework in which the base model itself acts as data generator, relevance annotator, and roll-out policy to synthesize streaming trajectories without external supervision. With only $1{,}000$ self-generated samples ($139\times$ less than the leading streaming instruction-tuning approach) and no architectural changes, EvoStreaming consistently improves the overall RealStreamEval score by up to $10.8$ points across five open VideoLLM backbones (Qwen2/2.5/3-VL, InternVL-3.5, MiniCPM-V4.5) while largely preserving offline video performance. These results suggest that data-efficient interaction tuning is a practical path for adapting existing VideoLLMs to streaming assistants.

</details>


### [198] [BGG: Bridging the Geometric Gap between Cross-View images by Vision Foundation Model Adaptation for Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10345)
*Wei Wang,Dou Quan,Ning Huyan,Shuang Wang,Yi Li,Pei He,Licheng Jiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BGG框架，基于视觉基础模型（如DINOv3）通过参数高效适配（MFEA模块和FASA模块）来桥接跨视图图像间的几何差异，在University-1652和SUES-200数据集上取得了最先进的跨视角地理定位性能，且训练成本低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨视角图像（如无人机与卫星图）之间的几何差异严重制约了跨视角地理定位（CVGL）的性能，现有方法难以高效弥合这一几何鸿沟。

Method: 提出BGG框架，包含两个核心模块：1）多粒度特征增强适配器（MFEA），通过多级膨胀卷积增强特征的尺度适应性和视角鲁棒性；2）频域感知结构聚合模块（FASA），在频域调制patch token并进行自适应聚合以增强局部结构特征，最终将增强的局部特征与[CLS] token融合进行定位。

Result: 在University-1652和SUES-200数据集上的大量实验表明，BGG相比其他方法具有显著优势，并以低训练成本实现了最先进的定位性能。

Conclusion: 基于视觉基础模型的参数高效适配框架能够有效桥接跨视图几何差异，在CVGL任务中兼顾高性能与低训练成本，验证了利用VFM泛化能力解决跨视图几何鸿沟的有效性。

Abstract: Geometric differences between cross-view images, such as drone and satellite views, significantly increase the challenge of Cross-View Geo-Localization (CVGL), which aims to acquire the geolocation of images by image retrieval. To further enhance the CVGL performance, this paper proposes a parameter-efficient adaptation framework for bridging the geometric gap across images based on the vision foundation model (VFM) (e.g., DINOv3), termed BGG. BGG not only effectively leverages the general visual representations of VFM and captures the robust and consistent features from cross-view images, but also utilizes the generalization capabilities of the VFM, significantly improving the CVGL performance. It mainly contains a Multi-granularity Feature Enhancement Adapter (MFEA) and a Frequency-Aware Structural Aggregation (FASA) module. Specifically, MFEA enhances the scale adaptability and viewpoint robustness of features by multi-level dilated convolutions, effectively bridging the cross-view geometric gap with small training costs. Additionally, considering the [CLS] token lacks spatial details for precise image retrieval and localization, the FASA module modulates patch tokens in the frequency domain and performs adaptive aggregation for local structural feature enhancement. Finally, BGG fuses the enhanced local features with the [CLS] token for more accurate CVGL. Extensive experiments on University-1652 and SUES-200 datasets demonstrate that BGG has significant advantages over other methods and achieves state-of-the-art localization performance with low training costs.

</details>


### [199] [Portable Active Learning for Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10349)
*Rashi Sharma,Justin Timothy C. Bersamin,Karthikk Subramanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PAL是一个检测器无关的主动学习框架，仅利用推理输出，通过类别级实例不确定性结合图像级多样性来选择数据批次，在多个数据集上显著提升标签效率和检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标注边界框成本高昂，限制了目标检测的可扩展性；现有主动学习方法依赖模型特征或修改检测器内部结构，集成开销大，且未能联合利用图像级信号、类别不平衡线索和实例级不确定性进行全面选择。

Method: PAL框架：1) 训练轻量级类别逻辑回归分类器区分真/假阳性，产生基于熵的不确定性分数；2) 结合全局图像熵、类别多样性和图像相似度对候选图像进行精炼，选出信息丰富且多样化的批次。整个方法仅需推理输出，无需修改检测器内部或训练流程。

Result: 在COCO、PASCAL VOC和BDD100K数据集上的大量实验表明，PAL在标签效率和检测精度上持续优于现有主动学习基线方法。

Conclusion: PAL是一种实用且可扩展的解决方案，适用于真实世界中目标检测的经济高效部署，具有良好的检测器兼容性和集成便捷性。

Abstract: Annotating bounding boxes is costly and limits the scalability of object detection. This challenge is compounded by the need to preserve high accuracy while minimizing manual effort in real-world applications. Prior active learning methods often depend on model features or modify detector internals and training schedules, increasing integration overhead. Moreover, they rarely jointly exploit the benefits of image-level signals, class-imbalance cues, and instance-level uncertainty for comprehensive selection. We present Portable Active Learning (PAL), a detector-agnostic, easily portable framework that operates solely on inference outputs. PAL combines class-wise instance uncertainty with image-level diversity to guide data selection. At each round, PAL trains lightweight class-specific logistic classifiers to distinguish true from false positives, producing entropy-based uncertainty scores for proposals. Candidate images are then refined using global image entropy, class diversity, and image similarity, yielding batches that are both informative and diverse. PAL requires no changes to model internals or training pipelines, ensuring broad compatibility across detectors. Extensive experiments on COCO, PASCAL VOC, and BDD100K demonstrate that PAL consistently improves label efficiency and detection accuracy compared to existing active learning baselines, making it a practical solution for scalable and cost-effective deployment of object detection in real-world settings.

</details>


### [200] [DySurface: Consistent 4D Surface Reconstruction via Bridging Explicit Gaussians and Implicit Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10360)
*Minje Kim,Younghyun Noh,Jaesoon Kim,Tae-Kyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DySurface提出了一种融合显式3D高斯与隐式有符号距离函数(SDF)的动态场景表面重建框架，通过VoxGS-DSDF分支利用变形高斯构建动态稀疏体素网格，为隐式SDF场提供显式几何引导，从而在保持渲染性能的同时显著提升动态场景的几何表面重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态场景新视角合成方法（NeRF和3DGS）虽渲染能力强，但仅依赖光度优化导致几何歧义，产生不连续表面、严重伪影和随时间断裂的表面问题。

Method: 提出DySurface框架，核心是VoxGS-DSDF分支：利用变形后的3D高斯构建动态稀疏体素网格，为隐式SDF场提供显式几何锚定，解决3DGS前向变形（规范→动态）与体SDF渲染所需反向变形（动态→规范）之间的结构差异。

Result: 在几何精度指标上显著优于最先进的基线方法，同时保持有竞争力的渲染性能，重建出具有水密边界和细节表示的表面。

Conclusion: 显式高斯与隐式SDF的有效桥接可以解决动态场景中的几何歧义问题，实现高质量的表面重建。

Abstract: While novel view synthesis (NVS) for dynamic scenes has seen significant progress, reconstructing temporally consistent geometric surfaces remains a challenge. Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) offer powerful dynamic scene rendering capabilities; however, relying solely on photometric optimization often leads to geometric ambiguities. This results in discontinuous surfaces, severe artifacts, and broken surfaces over time. To address these limitations, we present DySurface, a novel framework that bridges the effectiveness of explicit Gaussians with the geometric fidelity of implicit Signed Distance Functions (SDFs) in dynamic scenes. Our approach tackles the structural discrepancy between the forward deformation of 3DGS ($canonical \rightarrow dynamic$) and the backward deformation required for volumetric SDF rendering ($dynamic \rightarrow canonical$). Specifically, we propose the VoxGS-DSDF branch that leverages deformed Gaussians to construct a dynamic sparse voxel grid, providing explicit geometric guidance to the implicit SDF field. This explicit anchoring effectively regularizes the volumetric rendering process, significantly improving surface reconstruction quality, with watertight boundaries and detailed representations. Quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that DySurface significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in geometric accuracy metrics while maintaining competitive rendering performance.

</details>


### [201] [CellDX AI Autopilot: Agent-Guided Training and Deployment of Pathology Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10362)
*Alexey Pchelnikov,Aleksei Pchelnikov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CellDX AI Autopilot是一个通过自然语言交互实现全切片病理图像分类模型训练、评估和部署的平台，具备专用智能体技能架构和高效超参数搜索策略，支持非ML背景用户和ML从业者使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前计算病理学AI模型训练需要昂贵的全切片图像数据集、GPU基础设施、机器学习深度知识和大量工程投入，存在ML专业人才瓶颈和工程效率瓶颈，限制了诊断病理学的采纳和研究实验的效率。

Method: 构建结构化智能体技能体系，引导用户完成数据集整理、自动超参数调优、多策略模型比较和人机协同部署；基于超过32,000例病例和66,000张H&E染色全切片图像的预构建数据集及预提取特征；采用多重实例学习（MIL）训练框架支持四种分类策略；提出迭代配对超参数搜索（网格搜索或带种子的随机搜索），相比穷举搜索降低30倍以上调优成本；通过病理学专用技能和训练平台暴露给通用AI智能体（如任意基于LLM的智能体运行时）。

Result: 实现了端到端自动化模型训练，调优成本降低30倍以上；平台支持从无ML背景的病理学家到进行大量并行实验的ML从业者等不同用户群体；是首个将病理学专用智能体技能和训练平台暴露给通用AI智能体的系统。

Conclusion: CellDX AI Autopilot解决了ML专业知识瓶颈和工程效率瓶颈两大问题，降低了计算病理学模型开发的门槛和成本，有望推动诊断病理学领域AI应用的更广泛采纳。

Abstract: Training AI models for computational pathology currently requires access to expensive whole-slide-image datasets, GPU infrastructure, deep expertise in machine learning, and substantial engineering effort. We present CellDX AI Autopilot, a platform that lets users -- from pathologists with no ML background to ML practitioners running many parallel experiments -- train, evaluate, and deploy whole-slide image classifiers through natural language interaction with an AI agent. The platform provides a structured set of agent skills that guide the user through dataset curation, automated hyperparameter tuning, multi-strategy model comparison, and human-in-the-loop deployment, all on a pre-built dataset of over 32,000 cases and 66,000 H&E-stained whole-slide images with pre-extracted features. We describe the agent skill architecture, the underlying Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) training framework supporting four classification strategies, and an iterative pairwise hyperparameter search (grid or seeded random) that reduces tuning cost by over 30x compared to exhaustive search. CellDX AI Autopilot is, to our knowledge, the first system to expose pathology-specialized agent skills and a pathology-specialized training platform to general-purpose AI agents (e.g. any LLM-based agent runtime), delivering end-to-end automated model training without requiring the agent itself to be domain-specific. The platform addresses both the ML-expertise bottleneck that limits adoption in diagnostic pathology and the engineering bottleneck that limits how many experiments a researcher can run cost-effectively.

</details>


### [202] [SleepWalk: A Three-Tier Benchmark for Stress-Testing Instruction-Guided Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10376)
*Niyati Rawal,Sushant Ravva,Shah Alam Abir,Saksham Jain,Aman Chadha,Vinija Jain,Suranjana Trivedy,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SleepWalk是一个评估视觉语言模型在3D环境中进行指令引导轨迹预测的基准测试，包含2,472个3D场景和9条指令，覆盖三个难度层级。测试发现当前模型在空间推理（尤其遮挡、交互约束和多步指令）上存在系统性失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航基准多聚焦于跨房间长程探索，缺乏对局部、以交互为中心的具身推理的评估。研究者希望检验VLM能否在3D数字环境中将语言可靠地映射为空间连贯、可执行的行动轨迹。

Method: SleepWalk基准从文本场景描述生成单场景3D世界并过滤可导航性，提供渲染视觉观察和自然语言指令，要求模型预测尊重场景几何、避免碰撞并在动作兼容位置终止的轨迹。任务按空间和时间难度分为三层，采用标准化逐点评判评估协议对2,472个场景中的9条指令进行测试。

Result: 在三个前沿VLM上的评估显示：模型在接地空间推理上存在系统性失败，尤其在遮挡、交互约束和多步指令条件下，性能随任务难度增加而下降。当前VLM在一定程度上能产生空间连贯、可执行且与意图对齐的轨迹。

Conclusion: SleepWalk通过可控且可扩展的设置暴露了当前VLM在接地多模态推理、具身规划、视觉语言导航方面的失败，为推进这些方向以及构建可在3D环境中执行动作的智能体提供了关键基准。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have advanced rapidly in multimodal perception and language understanding, yet it remains unclear whether they can reliably ground language into spatially coherent, plausibly executable actions in 3D digital environments. We introduce SleepWalk, a benchmark for evaluating instruction-grounded trajectory prediction in single-scene 3D worlds generated from textual scene descriptions and filtered for navigability. Unlike prior navigation benchmarks centered on long-range exploration across rooms, SleepWalk targets localized, interaction-centric embodied reasoning: given rendered visual observations and a natural-language instruction, a model must predict a trajectory that respects scene geometry, avoids collisions, and terminates at an action-compatible location. The benchmark covers diverse indoor and outdoor environments and organizes tasks into three tiers of spatial and temporal difficulty, enabling fine-grained analysis of grounding under increasing compositional complexity. Using a standardized pointwise judge-based evaluation protocol, we evaluate three frontier VLMs on 2,472 curated 3D environments with nine instructions per scene. Results reveal systematic failures in grounded spatial reasoning, especially under occlusion, interaction constraints, and multi-step instructions: performance drops as the difficulty level of the tasks increase. In general, current VLMs can somewhat produce trajectories that are simultaneously spatially coherent, plausibly executable, and aligned with intended actions. By exposing failures in a controlled yet scalable setting, SleepWalk provides a critical benchmark for advancing grounded multimodal reasoning, embodied planning, vision-language navigation, and action-capable agents in 3D environments.

</details>


### [203] [Temporal Sampling Frequency Matters: A Capacity-Aware Study of End-to-End Driving Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10388)
*Yumao Liu,Tao Liu,Xiangyu Li,Jiaxiang Li,Ke Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文质疑自动驾驶轨迹预测中默认使用最高时间采样频率的假设，通过构建频率扫描训练集，发现模型性能与采样频率之间存在非单调关系：小模型在低频或中频表现更优，而大模型（AutoVLA）在最高频率表现最佳。研究建议应将时间采样频率作为可调超参数而非固定为最高值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"更高时间采样频率必然提升E2E自动驾驶轨迹预测性能"这一普遍假设，探究采样频率作为训练集设计变量的影响。

Method: 对高频E2E驾驶数据集进行时间下采样构建频率扫描训练集，在固定协议下训练评估同一模型，从容量感知角度分析频率响应；实验涵盖三个小模型和AutoVLA大模型，在Waymo、nuScenes、PAVE三个数据集上评估。

Result: 小模型在低频或中频达到最佳3秒ADE，呈现非单调或接近平台效应；AutoVLA在所有数据集上均在最高频率取得最佳ADE和FDE；迭代匹配控制实验表明小模型的优势不能仅归因于训练更新次数不等。

Conclusion: 时间采样频率应作为超参数报告和调优，而非固定为数据集最高可用频率；模型容量与最优采样频率之间存在依赖关系。

Abstract: End to end (E2E) autonomous driving trajectory prediction is often trained with camera frames sampled at the highest available temporal frequency, assuming that denser sampling improves performance. We question this assumption by treating temporal sampling frequency as an explicit training set design variable. Starting from high frequency E2E driving datasets, we construct frequency sweep training sets by temporally subsampling camera frames along each trajectory. For each model dataset pair, we train and evaluate the same model under a fixed protocol, so the frequency response reflects how prediction performance changes with sampling frequency. We analyze this response from a capacity aware perspective. Sparse sampling may miss driving relevant cues, while dense sampling may add redundant visual content and off manifold noise. For finite capacity models, this can create a driving irrelevant capacity burden. We evaluate three smaller E2E models and a larger VLA style AutoVLA model on Waymo, nuScenes, and PAVE. Results show model and dataset dependent frequency responses. Smaller E2E models often show non monotonic or near plateau trends and achieve their best 3 second ADE at lower or intermediate frequencies. In contrast, AutoVLA achieves its best 3 second ADE and FDE at the highest evaluated frequency on all three datasets. Iteration matched controls suggest that the advantage of lower or intermediate frequencies for smaller models is not explained only by unequal training update counts. These findings show that temporal sampling frequency should be reported and tuned, rather than fixed to the highest available value.

</details>


### [204] [Sens-VisualNews: A Benchmark Dataset for Sensational Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10394)
*Andreas Goulas,Damianos Galanopoulos,Evlampios Apostolidis,Vasileios Mezaris*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出“耸动图像检测”任务，构建了包含9,576张新闻图片的Sens-VisualNews基准数据集，并系统评估了多种多模态大语言模型在该任务上的零样本和微调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耸动性内容会绕过读者的批判性评估、加速病毒式传播，因此检测媒体中的耸动内容对于识别值得核查的信息和标记潜在的虚假信息至关重要。

Method: 1) 定义耸动图像检测任务；2) 构建Sens-VisualNews数据集（9,576张带标注的新闻图片）；3) 评估多种前沿多模态大语言模型在零样本和微调设置下的提示敏感性、性能和鲁棒性。

Result: 构建了包含9,576张图像的新基准数据集，并系统研究了多种多模态大语言模型在耸动图像检测上的表现。

Conclusion: 耸动图像检测可作为识别虚假信息和核查内容的关键过滤机制，现有模型在该任务上的表现值得进一步研究和优化。

Abstract: The detection of sensational content in media items can be a critical filtering mechanism for identifying check-worthy content and flagging potential disinformation, since such content triggers physiological arousal that often bypasses critical evaluation and accelerates viral sharing. In this paper we introduce the task of sensational image detection, which aims to determine whether an image contains shocking, provocative, or emotionally charged features to grab attention and trigger strong emotional responses. To support research on this task, we create a new benchmark dataset (called Sens-VisualNews) that contains 9,576 images from news items, annotated based on the (in-)existence of various sensational concepts and events in their visual content. Finally, using Sens-VisualNews, we study the prompt sensitivity, performance and robustness of a wide range of open SotA Multimodal LLMs, across both zero-shot and fine-tuned settings.

</details>


### [205] [AnomalyClaw: A Universal Visual Anomaly Detection Agent via Tool-Grounded Refutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10397)
*Xi Jiang,Yinjie Zhao,Zesheng Yang,Feng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AnomalyClaw，一种无需训练的多轮反驳式VAD智能体，将异常检测转化为候选-反驳过程，利用13种工具进行视觉验证和参考比对，在12个数据集上显著优于单步直接推理，并引入可选的自演化机制通过内部分支分歧构建规则库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨领域视觉异常检测面临定义、数据模态和标注标准差异，单领域模型迁移困难；单次推理的VLM依赖先验知识而非正常样本参考或细粒度特征证据，判断不可靠。

Method: 提出AnomalyClaw训练-free VAD智能体，将异常判断转化为多轮反驳过程：每轮智能体提出候选异常并与正常样本参考进行反驳；构建包含视觉验证、参考解析和冻结专家探测的13工具库；引入可选的口头自我演化扩展，通过内部分支分歧在线构建规则库，无需oracle标签。

Result: 在CrossDomainVAD-12基准（12个数据集）上，相比单步直接推理，AnomalyClaw在GPT-5.5上提升+6.23pp、Seed2.0-lite上+7.93pp、Qwen3.5-VL-27B上+3.52pp宏AUROC；自演化扩展在Qwen3.5-VL-27B上平均提升+2.09pp，与K=10 oracle标签监督基线（+1.99pp）相当。

Conclusion: 智能体式反驳过程能提升VLM对异常的理解和推理能力，而非简单聚合工具输出；自演化机制可在无监督条件下获得与有监督基线相当的性能提升。

Abstract: Visual anomaly detection (VAD) is crucial in many real-world fields, such as industrial inspection, medical imaging, infrastructure monitoring, and remote sensing. However, the specific anomaly definitions, data modalities, and annotation standards across different domains make it difficult to transfer single-domain trained VAD models. Vision-language models (VLMs), pre-trained on large-scale cross-domain data, can perform visual perception under task instructions, offering a promising solution for cross-domain VAD. However, single-inference VLM judgments are unreliable, since they rely more on prior knowledge than on normal-sample references or fine-grained feature evidence. We therefore present AnomalyClaw, a training-free VAD agent that turns anomaly judgment into a multi-round refutation process. In each round, the agent proposes candidate anomalies and refutes each against normal-sample references, drawing on a 13-tool library for visual verification, reference parsing, and frozen expert probing. On the CrossDomainVAD-12 benchmark (12 datasets), AnomalyClaw achieves consistent macro-AUROC improvements over single-step direct inference with +6.23 pp on GPT-5.5, +7.93 pp on Seed2.0-lite, and +3.52 pp on Qwen3.5-VL-27B. We further introduce an optional verbalized self-evolution extension. It builds an online rulebook from internal-branch disagreement without oracle labels. On Qwen3.5-VL-27B, it delivers a +2.09 pp mean gain, comparable to a K = 10 oracle-label supervised baseline (+1.99 pp). These results show that agentic refutation improve anomaly understanding and reasoning of VLMs, rather than merely aggregating tool outputs.

</details>


### [206] [Progressive Photorealistic Simplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10409)
*Adi Rosenthal,Dana Berman,Yedid Hoshen,Ariel Shamir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种渐进式语义图像简化框架，在保留照片真实感的同时，通过迭代移除和修复场景元素来简化图像，并进一步蒸馏为视频生成模型以直接预测简化序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像简化技术（如NPR）虽能降低视觉复杂度，但通常牺牲照片真实感。本文探索在保持照片真实感的前提下简化图像的互补方向。

Method: 采用迭代的Select-Remove-Verify流水线：利用视觉语言模型（VLM）识别和排序待移除元素，结合生成式编辑进行移除和修复，通过学习的验证器确保照片真实感和连贯性。此外，将这一过程蒸馏为图像到视频生成模型，直接预测连贯的简化序列。

Result: 能够生成更干净、更聚焦的构图，实现内容感知的去杂、语义层分解和交互式编辑等应用。

Conclusion: 通过结构化内容移除的简化方法可以在照片真实感领域作为引导视觉解释的实用机制，补充传统的抽象方法。

Abstract: Existing image simplification techniques often rely on Non-Photorealistic Rendering (NPR), transforming photographs into stylized sketches, cartoons, or paintings. While effective at reducing visual complexity, such approaches typically sacrifice photographic realism. In this work, we explore a complementary direction: simplifying images while preserving their photorealistic appearance. We introduce progressive semantic image simplification, a framework that iteratively reduces scene complexity by removing and inpainting elements in a controlled manner. At each step, the resulting image remains a plausible natural photograph. Our method combines semantic understanding with generative editing, leveraging Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to identify and prioritize elements for removal, and a learned verifier to ensure photorealism and coherence throughout the process. This is implemented via an iterative Select-Remove-Verify pipeline that produces high-quality simplification trajectories. To improve efficiency, we further distill this process into an image-to-video generation model that directly predicts coherent simplification sequences from a single input image. Beyond generating cleaner and more focused compositions, our approach enables applications such as content-aware decluttering, semantic layer decomposition, and interactive editing. More broadly, our work suggests that simplification through structured content removal can serve as a practical mechanism for guiding visual interpretation within the photorealistic domain, complementing traditional abstraction methods.

</details>


### [207] [CoWorld-VLA: Thinking in a Multi-Expert World Model for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10426)
*Minqing Huang,Yujiao Xiang,Zihan Liang,Jiajie Huang,Jingqi Wang,Zhi Xu,Feiyang Tan,Hangning Zhou,Mu Yang,Gong Che*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoWorld-VLA提出了一种多专家世界推理框架，通过语义交互、几何结构、动态演化和自我轨迹四种专家token作为显式条件，结合扩散层次化多专家融合规划器，实现连续时空结构保持的端到端自动驾驶规划，在NAVSIM v1基准上取得了竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的推理机制（文本CoT和潜在世界推理）难以提供保留连续时空结构的规划导向中间表示，导致无法直接作为动作生成的条件。

Method: 1) 多源监督提取互补世界信息并编码为四种专家token（语义交互、几何结构、动态演化、自我轨迹）；2) 扩散层次化多专家融合规划器，在联合去噪过程中耦合场景上下文以生成连续自我轨迹。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1基准上，未来场景生成和规划均取得竞争性结果，在碰撞避免和轨迹准确性方面表现强劲。

Conclusion: 专家token具有互补性，作为规划条件对动作生成有效；源码开源。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for end-to-end autonomous driving. However, existing reasoning mechanisms still struggle to provide planning-oriented intermediate representations: textual Chain-of-Thought (CoT) fails to preserve continuous spatiotemporal structure, while latent world reasoning remains difficult to use as a direct condition for action generation. In this paper, we propose CoWorld-VLA, a multi-expert world reasoning framework for autonomous driving, where world representations serve as explicit conditions to guide action planning. CoWorld-VLA extracts complementary world information through multi-source supervision and encodes it into expert tokens within the VLA, thereby providing planner-accessible conditioning signals. Specifically, we construct four types of tokens: semantic interaction, geometric structure, dynamic evolution, and ego trajectory tokens, which respectively model interaction intent, spatial structure, future temporal dynamics, and behavioral goals. During action generation, CoWorld-VLA employs a diffusion-based hierarchical multi-expert fusion planner, which is coupled with scene context throughout the joint denoising process to generate continuous ego trajectories. Experiments show that CoWorld-VLA achieves competitive results in both future scene generation and planning on the NAVSIM v1 benchmark, demonstrating strong performance in collision avoidance and trajectory accuracy. Ablation studies further validate the complementarity of expert tokens and their effectiveness as planning conditions for action generation. Code will be available at https://github.com/potatochip1211/CoWorld-VLA.

</details>


### [208] [WorldReasonBench: Human-Aligned Stress Testing of Video Generators as Future World-State Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10434)
*Keming Wu,Yijing Cui,Wenhan Xue,Qijie Wang,Xuan Luo,Zhiyuan Feng,Zuhao Yang,Sudong Wang,Sicong Jiang,Haowei Zhu,Zihan Wang,Ping Nie,Wenhu Chen,Bin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldReasonBench是一个评估视频生成模型世界推理能力的基准，包含436个测试用例和4个推理维度，通过Process-aware Reasoning Verification和Multi-dimensional Quality Assessment两阶段方法进行评估，并引入WorldRewardBench偏好基准。实验发现当前视频生成模型在视觉上逼真但在世界推理上存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然视频生成系统进步迅速被视为"世界模拟器"，但社区缺乏直接测试模型是否能推理观察世界应如何随时间演变的基准。

Method: 将视频生成评估重新定义为世界状态预测：给定初始状态和动作，模型能否生成状态演化在物理、社会、逻辑和信息上一致的未来视频。包含436个测试用例（4个推理维度、22个子类别），采用两阶段评估方法：1）Process-aware Reasoning Verification（结构化QA和推理阶段诊断）；2）Multi-dimensional Quality Assessment（推理质量、时间一致性、视觉美感）。引入WorldRewardBench（约6K专家标注对、1.4K视频）。

Result: 现代视频生成模型在视觉逼真度和世界推理之间存在持续差距：视频看起来令人信服，但在动力学、因果关系或信息保留方面失败。

Conclusion: 基准和评估工具将开源以支持社区研究真正具有世界意识的视频生成。

Abstract: Commercial video generation systems such as Seedance2.0 and Veo3.1 have rapidly improved, strengthening the view that video generators may be evolving into "world simulators." Yet the community still lacks a benchmark that directly tests whether a model can reason about how an observed world should evolve over time. We introduce WorldReasonBench, which reframes video generation evaluation as world-state prediction: given an initial state and an action, can a model generate a future video whose state evolution remains physically, socially, logically, and informationally consistent? WorldReasonBench contains 436 curated test cases with structured ground-truth QA annotations spanning four reasoning dimensions and 22 subcategories. We evaluate generated videos with a human-aligned two-part methodology: Process-aware Reasoning Verification uses structured QA and reasoning-phase diagnostics to detect temporal and causal failures, while Multi-dimensional Quality Assessment scores reasoning quality, temporal consistency, and visual aesthetics for ranking and reward modeling. We further introduce WorldRewardBench, a preference benchmark with approximately 6K expert-annotated pairs over 1.4K videos, supporting pair-wise and point-wise reward-model evaluation. Across modern video generators, our results expose a persistent gap between visual plausibility and world reasoning: videos can look convincing while failing dynamics, causality, or information preservation. We will release our benchmarks and evaluation toolkit to support community research on genuinely world-aware video generation at https://github.com/UniX-AI-Lab/WorldReasonBench/.

</details>


### [209] [Filtering Memorization from Parameter-Space in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10439)
*Yu Zhe,Yang Jiayan,Wei Junhao,Yu-Lin Tsai,Wang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Base-Anchored Filtering (BAF)方法，通过将LoRA更新分解为频谱通道并测量其与预训练主干主子空间的对齐程度，在无需训练和数据的情况下，对扩散模型LoRA进行后置记忆缓解，既减少记忆化又保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA在扩散模型定制化中广泛应用，但会记忆训练图像，导致生成输出复制版权或敏感内容。在LoRA共享生态中，用户只发布LoRA权重而不公开训练数据，现有缓解记忆的方法需要访问训练流程、训练数据或控制推理过程，难以适用于仅有LoRA权重的情况。

Method: 提出Base-Anchored Filtering (BAF)——一种无训练、无数据的后置记忆缓解框架。将LoRA更新分解为频谱通道，测量其与预训练主干主特征子空间的对齐程度；强对齐的通道被保留为可泛化适应，弱对齐的通道被抑制为潜在的记忆内容载体。

Result: 在多个数据集和扩散主干的实验表明，BAF持续减少记忆化，同时保持甚至提升生成质量。

Conclusion: BAF为LoRA共享生态中仅有权重可访问时的记忆化缓解提供了一种有效的后处理方案。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become a widely used mechanism for customizing diffusion models, enabling users to inject new visual concepts or styles through lightweight parameter updates. However, LoRAs can memorize training images, causing generated outputs to reproduce copyrighted or sensitive content. This risk is particularly concerning in LoRA-sharing ecosystems, where users distribute trained LoRAs without releasing the underlying training data. Existing approaches for mitigating memorization rely on access to the training pipeline, training data, or control over the inference process, making them difficult to apply when only the released LoRA weights are available. We propose \textbf{Base-Anchored Filtering (BAF)}, a training-free and data-free framework for post-hoc memorization mitigation in diffusion LoRAs. BAF decomposes LoRA updates into spectral channels and measures their alignment with the principal subspace of the pretrained backbone. Channels strongly aligned with this subspace are retained as generalizable adaptations, while weakly aligned channels are suppressed as potential carriers of memorized content. Experiments on multiple datasets and diffusion backbones demonstrate that BAF consistently reduces memorization while preserving or even improving generation quality. Our code is available in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [210] [Uni-Synergy: Bridging Understanding and Generation for Personalized Reasoning via Co-operative Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10445)
*Zijun Shen,Sihan Yang,Ruichuan An,Ziyu Guo,Hao Liang,Ming Lu,Renrui Zhang,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sync-R1是一个端到端强化学习框架，通过统一的显式推理循环联合优化多模态模型的个性化理解与生成任务，采用Sync-GRPO集成奖励方法和动态组缩放（DGS）技术，在UnifyBench++数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型（UMMs）在通用任务上表现优异，但在个性化理解与生成之间的鸿沟上仍存在不足。现有工作主要依赖监督微调下的隐式token级对齐，未能充分捕捉理解与创造之间的潜在协同效应。

Method: 提出Sync-R1框架，核心包含三个组件：(1) Sync-GRPO，一种利用集成奖励系统的强化学习方法；(2) 统一的显式推理循环，使个性化理解指导内容生成，同时生成质量反向优化理解；(3) 动态组缩放（DGS），自适应过滤低潜力轨迹以减少梯度方差并加速收敛。同时构建UnifyBench++数据集，包含更密集的文本描述和更丰富的用户上下文。

Result: Sync-R1达到最先进的性能，展现出卓越的跨任务推理能力和鲁棒个性化能力，且无需复杂的冷启动流程。

Conclusion: 本文验证了通过强化学习框架统一优化个性化理解与生成任务的有效性，证明了显式推理循环和集成奖励机制能够充分挖掘跨任务协同潜力。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) excel in general tasks but struggle to bridge the gap between personalized understanding and generation. Prior works largely rely on implicit token-level alignment via supervised fine-tuning, which fails to fully capture the potential synergy between comprehension and creation. In this work, we propose Sync-R1, an end-to-end reinforcement learning framework that jointly optimizes personalized understanding and generation within a single, explicit reasoning loop. Through this unified feedback process, Sync-R1 enables personalized comprehension to guide content creation, while the resulting generation quality reciprocally refines understanding within an integrated reward landscape. To efficiently orchestrate this dual-task synergy, we introduce Sync-GRPO, a reinforcement learning method utilizing an ensemble reward system. Furthermore, we propose Dynamic Group Scaling (DGS), which adaptively filters low-potential trajectories to reduce gradient variance and accelerate convergence. To better reflect real-world complexity, we introduce UnifyBench++, featuring denser textual descriptions and richer user contexts. Experimental results demonstrate that Sync-R1 achieves state-of-the-art performance, showcasing superior cross-task reasoning and robust personalization without requiring complex cold-start procedures. The code and the UnifyBench++ dataset will be released at: https://github.com/arctanxarc/UniCTokens.

</details>


### [211] [Automated high-frequency quantification of fish communities and biomass using computer vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10449)
*Kota Ishikawa,Takuma Masui,Keita Koeda,Rickdane Gomez,Lucas Yutaka Kimura,Michio Kondoh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于计算机视觉的自动化框架，通过立体摄像系统和深度学习技术，从水下视频中量化鱼类群落的物种级丰度和生物量。在20天的逐时观测中，该方法揭示了鱼类群落结构的动态变化，并与传统视觉普查和eDNA调查相比，在持续、非侵入性定量监测方面具有互补优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鱼类群落调查方法（如捕捞、水下视觉普查、环境DNA宏条形码）要么劳动强度大，要么缺乏可靠的丰度和生物量估计，无法提供高频、定量的观测数据，亟需一种自动化、非侵入性的定量监测方法。

Method: 使用定制立体摄像系统采集水下视频，集成深度学习鱼类识别、多目标跟踪和三维重建技术，实现物种级丰度和生物量的自动估算。

Result: 在20天内对珊瑚礁鱼类群落进行逐时白天观测，成功揭示了物种丰富度、丰度和生物量的动态波动及其与物种组成变化的关系；与视觉普查和eDNA调查相比，该方法在持续监测一致观测到的物种方面具有互补优势。

Conclusion: 该方法为长期监测提供了可扩展的基础，提升了解析鱼类群落精细尺度时间动态的能力。

Abstract: Quantifying fish community structure is essential for understanding biodiversity and ecosystem responses in a changing environment, yet existing survey methods provide limited high-frequency, quantitative observations. Conventional approaches, including catch-based methods, underwater visual censuses, and environmental DNA metabarcoding, either require intensive labor or lack reliable estimates of abundance and biomass. Here, we develop an automated framework for quantifying fish communities from underwater video using computer vision. Using videos acquired with a custom-made stereo camera system, the framework integrates deep learning-based fish identification, multi-object tracking, and 3D reconstruction to estimate species-level abundance and biomass. We applied the approach to a reef fish community over a 20-day period with hourly daytime observations, revealing dynamic fluctuations in species richness, abundance, and biomass associated with changes in species composition. By comparing fish communities estimated from visual census and environmental DNA surveys, we demonstrate that our method provides complementary strengths for continuous, non-invasive, and quantitative monitoring of consistently observed species. This approach provides a scalable foundation for long-term monitoring and advances the capacity to resolve fine-scale temporal dynamics in fish communities.

</details>


### [212] [Automated Detection of Abnormalities in Zebrafish Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10464)
*Sarath Sivaprasad,Hui-Po Wang,Anna-Lisa Jäckel,Jonas Baumann,Carole Baumann,Jennifer Herrmann,Mario Fritz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个大规模斑马鱼胚胎发育显微图像数据集，并首次引入基于Transformer的时空特征模型，在受精率分类（98%准确率）和毒性评估（92%准确率）两项基准任务上取得了优异表现，有望推动斑马鱼胚胎毒性分析的自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斑马鱼胚胎是药物发现的重要模型，但现有评估依赖人工检测，成本高且劳动密集。机器学习虽有自动化潜力，但缺乏全面的数据集限制了进展。

Method: 构建大规模高分辨率显微图像序列数据集，包含对照和3,4-二氯苯胺暴露条件下的斑马鱼胚胎发育过程，并提供了专家精细时间粒度的标注。提出首个基于Transformer的基线模型，整合时空特征以早期预测发育异常。

Result: 在受精率分类任务（130,368张图像）上达到98%准确率，在毒性评估任务（55,296张图像）上达到92%准确率。

Conclusion: 自动化方法在增强基于斑马鱼的毒性分析方面具有巨大潜力，所提出的数据集和模型为后续研究提供了基准。

Abstract: Zebrafish embryos are a valuable model for drug discovery due to their optical transparency and genetic similarity to humans. However, current evaluations rely on manual inspection, which is costly and labor-intensive. While machine learning offers automation potential, progress is limited by the lack of comprehensive datasets. To address this, we introduce a large-scale dataset of high-resolution microscopic image sequences capturing zebrafish embryonic development under both control conditions and exposure to compounds (3,4-dichloroaniline). This dataset, with expert annotations at fine-grained temporal levels, supports two benchmarking tasks: (1) fertility classification, assessing zebrafish egg viability (130,368 images), and (2) toxicity assessment, detecting malformations induced by toxic exposure over time (55,296 images). Alongside the dataset, we present the first transformer-based baseline model that integrates spatiotemporal features to predict developmental abnormalities at early stages. Experimental results present the model's effectiveness, achieving 98% accuracy in fertility classification and 92% in toxicity assessment. These findings underscore the potential of automated approaches to enhance zebrafish-based toxicity analysis.

</details>


### [213] [Adaptive Context Matters: Towards Provable Multi-Modality Guidance for Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10470)
*Jinyi Luo,Minghao Liu,Yifan Li,Zejia Fan,Jiaying Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次对多模态超分辨率进行理论建模，揭示了现有方法存在模态利用次优的瓶颈，并提出M³ESR框架，通过动态模态融合和温度调度机制实现风险控制与语义一致性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超分辨率是严重不适定问题，现有语义引导和多模态SR方法虽利用大模型或外部先验增强语义对齐，但异质模态融合在实践中和理论上均未被充分理解，缺乏理论建模。

Method: 提出M³ESR框架，包含空间动态模态加权模块和时域自适应模态温度调度机制，实现空间-时间维度的自适应模态加权，用于精确风险控制和模态贡献优化。

Result: 实验表明M³ESR显著提升泛化性能和语义一致性，证实了方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析揭示模态权重与有效贡献对齐以及降低表示复杂度可改进泛化风险界，并据此设计的动态模态融合框架有效提升了多模态SR的性能。

Abstract: Super-resolution (SR) is a severely ill-posed problem with inherent ambiguity, as widely recognized in both empirical and theoretical studies. Although recent semantic-guided and multi-modal SR methods exploit large models or external priors to enhance semantic alignment, the fusion of heterogeneous modalities remains insufficiently understood in practice and theory. In this work, we provide the first theoretical modeling of multi-modal SR, revealing that prior methods are bottlenecked by sub-optimal modality utilization. Our analysis shows that the generalization risk bound can be improved by strengthening the alignment between modality weights and their effective contributions, while reducing representation complexity. This theoretical insight inspires us to propose the novel Multi-Modal Mixture-of-Experts Super-Resolution framework (M$^3$ESR) that employs generalization-oriented dynamic modality fusion for accurate risk control and modality contribution optimization. In detail, we propose a novel spatially dynamic modality weighting module and a temporally adaptive modality temperature scheduling mechanism, enabling flexible and adaptive spatial-temporal modality weighting for effective risk control. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our M$^3$ESR significantly boosts generalization and semantic consistency performances, which confirms our superiority.

</details>


### [214] [OpenSGA: Efficient 3D Scene Graph Alignment in the Open World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10484)
*Gang Chen,Sebastián Barbas Laina,Stefan Leutenegger,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的3D场景图对齐框架OpenSGA，融合视觉-语言、文本和几何特征，实现了帧到扫描和子扫描到子扫描两种对齐任务，并发布了包含超70万样本的大型数据集ScanNet-SG，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要聚焦于子扫描到子扫描的对齐，严重依赖几何点云特征，而帧到扫描对齐和开放集视觉-语言特征尚未被充分探索；同时现有数据集规模小、物体类别有限，限制了系统的训练与评估。

Method: 提出统一框架，融合视觉-语言、文本和几何特征与空间上下文；包含距离门控空间注意力编码器、基于最小费用流的分配器和全局场景嵌入生成器，以在大坐标差异下实现精确对齐；并设计了自动化标注管道生成大规模数据集ScanNet-SG。

Result: 在帧到扫描和子扫描到子扫描任务上均取得最佳性能，显著超越现有场景图对齐方法；数据集包含超70万样本，覆盖ScanNet的509个类别及GPT-4o标记的3000+类别。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效统一了两种对齐任务，开放集视觉-语言特征的引入和数据规模的扩大显著提升了场景图对齐的性能，有望支持长期记忆和大范围多智能体地图融合等机器人应用。

Abstract: Scene graph alignment establishes object correspondences between two 3D scene graphs constructed from partially overlapping observations. This enables efficient scene understanding and object-level relocalization when a robot revisits a place, as well as global map fusion across multiple agents. Such capabilities are essential for robots that require long-term memory for long-horizon tasks involving interactions with the environment. Existing approaches mainly focus on subscan-to-subscan (S2S) alignment and depend heavily on geometric point-cloud features, leaving frame-to-scan (F2S) alignment and open-set vision-language features underexplored. In addition, existing datasets for scene graph alignment remain small-scale with limited object diversity, constraining systematic training and evaluation. We present a unified and efficient scene graph alignment framework that predicts object correspondences by fusing vision-language, textual, and geometric features with spatial context. The framework comprises modules such as a distance-gated spatial attention encoder, a minimum-cost-flow-based allocator, and a global scene embedding generator to achieve accurate alignment even under large coordinate discrepancies. We further introduce ScanNet-SG, a large-scale dataset generated via an automated annotation pipeline with over 700k samples, covering 509 object categories from ScanNet labels and over 3k categories from GPT-4o-based tagging. Experiments show that our method achieves the best overall performance on both F2S and S2S tasks, substantially outperforming existing scene graph alignment methods. Our code and dataset are released at: https://autonomousrobots.nl/paper_websites/opensga.

</details>


### [215] [M$^2$E-UAV: A Benchmark and Analysis for Onboard Motion-on-Motion Event-Based Tiny UAV Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10496)
*Weiqi Yan,Lixin Chen,Xiangrui Hou,Zhipeng Cai,Youbiao Wang,Yangyang Shi,Yu Zang,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了M²E-UAV基准数据集和M²E-Point基线方法，用于解决事件相机在观测者和目标同时运动（motion-on-motion）场景下的微型无人机检测问题。M²E-Point将事件编码为点集，利用EdgeConv提取局部结构，通过DBSCAN生成检测框。实验表明，点基事件建模是强基线，而简单IMU条件化仅带来边际增益。在验证集上，M²E-Point达到0.9673 F1和0.5501 mAP50-95。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机在运动-运动（motion-on-motion）场景下进行微型无人机检测时，自运动会在建筑物、植被、地平线等背景上激活大量边缘事件，而无人机仅表现为稀疏事件簇，使得检测极具挑战。现有工作缺乏针对这一实际问题的基准和基线方法。

Method: 提出了M²E-UAV基准数据集（包含87,223训练样本和21,395验证样本，覆盖四种场景族），以及两个基线方法：（1）M²E-Point：将事件编码为[x,y,t,p]点集，通过EdgeConv提取局部事件结构，预测事件级无人机前景分数，再经DBSCAN聚类生成边界框；（2）M²E-Point+IMU：在M²E-Point基础上引入惯性测量单元（IMU）条件化。

Result: 在验证集上，M²E-Point达到0.9673 F1和0.5501 mAP50-95；IMU条件化变体达0.5561 mAP50-95，仅带来边际提升。

Conclusion: 点基事件建模是运动-运动场景下事件相机无人机检测的强基线；简单IMU条件化仅提供边际增益，该领域仍需进一步探索。

Abstract: Tiny UAV detection from an onboard event camera is difficult when the observer and target move at the same time. In this motion-on-motion regime, ego-motion activates background edges across buildings, vegetation, and horizon structures, while the UAV may appear as a sparse event cluster. To explore this practical problem, we present M$^2$E-UAV, a benchmark and analysis setup for onboard motion-on-motion event-based tiny UAV detection. The processed M$^2$E-UAV benchmark contains 87,223 training samples and 21,395 validation samples across four scene families: sunny building-forest, sunny farm-village, sunset building-forest, and sunset farm-village. We provide M$^2$E-Point, a point-based event baseline, and M$^2$E-Point + IMU, an IMU-conditioned variant, to analyze the role of inertial cues under onboard motion-on-motion detection.
